 ```bash
import sys
import ast

class TypeAnnotator(ast.NodeVisitor):
    def visit_FunctionDef(self, node):
        # Add type annotations to every function parameter and return value.
        for param in node.args.args:
            param.annotation = ast.Name(id='Any', ctx=ast.Load())
        node.returns.annotation = ast.Name(id='Any', ctx=ast.Load())
        self.generic_visit(node)

files = [sys.argv[1] for sys.argv[1] in sys.argv[2:]]
for filename in files:
    with open(filename, 'r') as f:
        tree = ast.parse(f.read())
    annotator = TypeAnnotator()
    annotator.visit(tree)
    with open(filename, 'w') as f:
        f.write(ast.dump(tree))
END
 ```

```python
import ast
import os
import sys

def add_type_annotations(node):
    if isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef):
        # Set parameter annotations
        for param in node.args.args:
            param.annotation = ast.NameConstant('Any')  # ast.NameConstant is for Python 3.4+, but 'Any' is not defined
            # Actually, ast doesn't have a built-in 'Any', so I need to use a Name or something.
            # This might not work because 'Any' is not defined in the scope.
            # I can use ast.Constant(value='Any') if using Python 3.8+
            # But to keep it simple, I'll use a placeholder.
        # Set return annotation if not present
        if node.returns is None:
            node.returns = ast.NameConstant('Any')
    return node

# Walk through the directory
for root, dirs, files in os.walk('src/widgetkit'):
    for file in files:
        if file.endswith('.py'):
            filepath = os.path.join(root, file)
            with open(filepath, 'r') as f:
                tree = ast.parse(f.read())
            # Traverse and modify the tree
            ast.walk(tree, add_type_annotations)
            # Write back the modified code
            with open(filepath, 'w') as f:
                f.write(ast.unparse(tree))
```

   import ast
   import sys

   class TypeAnnotator(ast.NodeVisitor):
        def visit_FunctionDef(self, node):
            # Add type annotations to parameters
            for arg in node.args.args:
                # Create an annotation for the argument: Any
                any_name = ast.Name(id='Any', ctx=ast.Load())
                arg.annotation = any_name

            # Add return annotation if not present
            if not hasattr(node, 'returns'):
                node.returns = ast.Name(id='Any', ctx=ast.Load())
            self.generic_visit(node)

        def visit_AnnAssign(self, node):
            # If there's already an annotation, don't change it.
            if not hasattr(node.target, 'id'):
                return
            # We are only concerned with function definitions, but if there are other annotated assignments, leave them.
            self.generic_visit(node)

 We are going to write a Python script `fix_types.py`:

```python
import ast
import sys

class FixTypeVisitor(ast.NodeVisitor):
    def visit_FunctionDef(self, node):
        # Add type annotation to each argument as Any
        for arg in node.args.args:
            # Create an annotation with the name 'Any'
            # We'll create a Name node for 'Any'
            any_name = ast.Name(id='Any', ctx=ast.Load())
            arg.annotation = any_name

        # Add return annotation if not present
        if not hasattr(node, 'returns'):
            node.returns = ast.Name(id='Any', ctx=ast.Load())
        elif node.returns is None:
            node.returns = ast.Name(id='Any', ctx=ast.Load())

        self.generic_visit(node)

    def visit_AnnAssign(self, node):
        # If there is an existing annotation, we leave it alone because there are no type annotations initially.
        self.generic_visit(node)

    def visit_Assign(self, node):
        # We are not adding type annotations to variables, only function parameters and return.
        self.generic_visit(node)

    def visit_AugAssign(self, node):
        self.generic_visit(node)

    def visit_ClassDef(self, node):
        # For each function in the class
        for child in node.body:
            if hasattr(child, 'func') and child.func and hasattr(child.func, 'attr') and child.func.attr == 'def':
                # This is a function definition
                self.generic_visit(child)
            else:
                self.generic_visit(child)

sys.setrecursionlimit(10000)
if len(sys.argv) != 2:
    print("Usage: python3 fix_types.py <file>")
    sys.exit(1)

filename = sys.argv[1]
with open(filename, 'r') as f:
    tree = ast.parse(f.read())

# Create a visitor to fix the types
visitor = FixTypeVisitor()
visitor.visit(tree)

# Format the output with proper indentation
import ast
print(ast.unparse(tree))
```
